[19768]     }

[19774]     Texture2D<float4> currentFrame  : register(t0);
[19775]     Texture2D<float4> previousFrame : register(t1);
[19776]     Texture2D<float>  currentDepth  : register(t2);
[19777]     Texture2D<float>  previousDepth : register(t3);
[19778]     RWTexture2D<float> reactiveOut  : register(u0);
[19779]     float getLuma(float3 c) {
[19780]         return dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
[19781]     }
[19782]     float colorDelta(float3 a, float3 b) {
[19783]         float3 aY = float3(
[19784]              0.25 * a.r + 0.5 * a.g + 0.25 * a.b,
[19785]              0.5  * a.r             - 0.5  * a.b,
[19786]             -0.25 * a.r + 0.5 * a.g - 0.25 * a.b);
[19787]         float3 bY = float3(
[19788]              0.25 * b.r + 0.5 * b.g + 0.25 * b.b,
[19789]              0.5  * b.r             - 0.5  * b.b,
[19790]             -0.25 * b.r + 0.5 * b.g - 0.25 * b.b);
[19791]         float3 d = abs(aY - bY);
[19792]         return d.x * 1.0 + (d.y + d.z) * 0.5;
[19793]     }
[19794]     // Vegetation confidence from chrominance: 0 = not vegetation, 1 = high confidence
[19795]     float vegetationConf(float3 c) {
[19796]         float luma = getLuma(c);
[19797]         if (luma < 0.02) return 0.0;
[19798]         // Green dominance normalized by brightness
[19799]         float greenDom = c.g - max(c.r, c.b);
[19800]         float greenRatio = greenDom / max(luma, 0.01);
[19801]         // Yellow-green (autumn leaves): g > b, r close to g
[19802]         float yellowGreen = (c.g > c.b * 1.2 && c.r > c.b * 1.1 && c.g > 0.08) ? 0.25 : 0.0;
[19803]         return saturate(greenRatio * 2.5 + yellowGreen);
[19804]     }
[19805]     [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
[19806]     void main(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
[19807]         uint2 dim;
[19808]         currentFrame.GetDimensions(dim.x, dim.y);
[19809]         if (id.x >= dim.x || id.y >= dim.y) return;
[19810]         float3 cur  = currentFrame[id.xy].rgb;
[19811]         float3 prev = previousFrame[id.xy].rgb;
[19812]         // --- Signal 1: Center color delta ---
[19813]         float delta = colorDelta(cur, prev);
[19814]         float curLuma = getLuma(cur);
[19815]         // --- Signal 2 + 3: Neighborhood max delta AND spatial variance (single pass) ---
[19816]         float maxNeighborDelta = delta;
[19817]         float spatialVar = 0.0;
[19818]         [unroll]
[19819]         for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
[19820]             [unroll]
[19821]             for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
[19822]                 if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
[19823]                 int2 npos = clamp(int2(id.xy) + int2(dx, dy), int2(0,0), int2(dim) - 1);
[19824]                 float3 nc = currentFrame[npos].rgb;
[19825]                 float3 np = previousFrame[npos].rgb;
[19826]                 maxNeighborDelta = max(maxNeighborDelta, colorDelta(nc, np));
[19827]                 float diff = curLuma - getLuma(nc);
[19828]                 spatialVar += diff * diff;
[19829]             }
[19830]         }
[19831]         float colorSignal = lerp(delta, maxNeighborDelta, 0.25);
[19832]         // Spatial detail: 0..1 (how much fine detail surrounds this pixel)
[19833]         float detail = saturate(sqrt(spatialVar / 8.0) * 8.0);
[19834]         // --- Signal 4: Depth discontinuity ---
[19835]         float depthCur  = currentDepth[id.xy];
[19836]         float depthPrev = previousDepth[id.xy];
[19837]         float depthSignal = saturate(abs(depthCur - depthPrev) * 20.0);
[19838]         // --- Signal 5: Vegetation detection ---
[19839]         float vegConf = vegetationConf(cur);
[19840]         // --- Adaptive thresholds per material ---
[19841]         // Base: general purpose
[19842]         float threshold = 0.015;
[19843]         float scale = 8.0;
[19844]         float power = 0.6;
[19845]         // Vegetation: much more sensitive (leaves sway subtly, branches flicker)
[19846]         threshold = lerp(threshold, 0.005, vegConf);   // 0.015 
[19847]  0.005
[19848]         scale     = lerp(scale,     14.0,  vegConf);   // 8 
[19849]         power     = lerp(power,     0.4,   vegConf);   // 0.6 
[19850]  0.4
[19851]         // High-frequency detail: also boost (thin branches, wires, grass blades)
[19852]         threshold *= (1.0 - detail * 0.3);  // up to 30% lower threshold
[19853]         scale     *= (1.0 + detail * 0.25); // up to 25% higher scale
[19854]         // --- Combine ---
[19855]         float colorReactive = saturate((colorSignal - threshold) * scale);
[19856]         colorReactive = pow(colorReactive, power);
[19857]         float reactive = max(colorReactive, depthSignal);
[19858]         // Vegetation baseline: even "static" vegetation in detailed areas gets
[19859]         // a minimum reactivity to prevent over-accumulation 
[19860]  sharper foliage
[19861]         float vegBaseline = vegConf * detail * 0.12;
[19862]         reactive = max(reactive, vegBaseline);
[19863]         reactiveOut[id.xy] = saturate(reactive);
[19864]     }
[19865]     Texture2D<float4> currentFrame  : register(t0);
[19866]     Texture2D<float4> previousFrame : register(t1);
[19867]     RWTexture2D<float> tcOut        : register(u0);
[19868]     float getLuma(float3 c) {
[19869]         return dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
[19870]     }
[19871]     [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
[19872]     void main(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
[19873]         uint2 dim;
[19874]         currentFrame.GetDimensions(dim.x, dim.y);
[19875]         if (id.x >= dim.x || id.y >= dim.y) {
[19876]             return;
[19877]         }
[19878]         float3 cur  = currentFrame[id.xy].rgb;
[19879]         float3 prev = previousFrame[id.xy].rgb;
[19880]         // Detect pixels where color changed but surrounding area is stable
[19881]         // This pattern is typical of particles, glass reflections, HUD
[19882]         float centerDelta = abs(getLuma(cur) - getLuma(prev));
[19883]         float neighborStable = 0.0;
[19884]         float count = 0.0;
[19885]         [unroll]
[19886]         for (int dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy += 2) {
[19887]             [unroll]
[19888]             for (int dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx += 2) {
[19889]                 if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
[19890]                 int2 npos = clamp(int2(id.xy) + int2(dx, dy), int2(0,0), int2(dim) - 1);
[19891]                 float3 nc = currentFrame[npos].rgb;
[19892]                 float3 np = previousFrame[npos].rgb;
[19893]                 float nd = abs(getLuma(nc) - getLuma(np));
[19894]                 neighborStable += step(nd, 0.02); // count stable neighbors
[19895]                 count += 1.0;
[19896]             }
[19897]         }
[19898]         // If center changed but neighbors are stable 
[19899]  likely transparency/particle
[19900]         float stability = neighborStable / max(count, 1.0);
[19901]         float tc = centerDelta * stability * 4.0;
[19902]         tcOut[id.xy] = saturate(tc);
[19903]     }

[20078]     [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
[20079]     void main(uint3 tid : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
[20080]         uint2 pixelCoord = tid.xy;
[20081]         float2 size = renderSize.xy;
[20082]         if (pixelCoord.x >= (uint)size.x || pixelCoord.y >= (uint)size.y) {
[20083]             return;
[20084]         }
[20085]         float fallback = params.z;
[20086]         // In fallback mode (no VP matrices), output zero motion
[20087]         if (fallback > 0.5) {
[20088]             MotionOutput[pixelCoord] = float2(0.0, 0.0);
[20089]             return;
[20090]         }
[20091]         // Read current depth (0..1, may be reversed)
[20092]         float depth = CurrentDepth.Load(int3(pixelCoord, 0));
[20093]         // Convert pixel coordinate to NDC [-1,1]
[20094]         float2 uv = (float2(pixelCoord) + 0.5) * renderSize.zw; // [0,1]
[20095]         float2 ndc = uv * 2.0 - 1.0;
[20096]         ndc.y = -ndc.y; // Flip Y for D3D convention
[20097]         // Reconstruct world position using inverse VP
[20098]         float4 clipPos = float4(ndc, depth, 1.0);
[20099]         float4 worldPos = mul(currentInvVP, clipPos);
[20100]         worldPos /= worldPos.w;
[20101]         // Reproject to previous frame
[20102]         float4 prevClip = mul(previousVP, worldPos);
[20103]         float2 prevNDC = prevClip.xy / prevClip.w;
[20104]         // Convert back to pixel space
[20105]         float2 prevUV = (prevNDC * float2(1, -1) + 1.0) * 0.5;
[20106]         float2 prevPixel = prevUV * size;
[20107]         float2 currentPixel = float2(pixelCoord) + 0.5;
[20108]         // Motion vector = previous position - current position (in pixels)
[20109]         // FSR expects motion from current to previous frame
[20110]         float2 motion = prevPixel - currentPixel;
[20111]         // Clamp to reasonable range (reject outliers from depth discontinuities)
[20112]         float maxMotion = max(size.x, size.y) * 0.25;
[20113]         float motionLen = length(motion);
[20114]         if (motionLen > maxMotion) {
[20115]             motion *= maxMotion / motionLen;
[20116]         }
[20117]         // Validate: if depth is at far plane (1.0 or 0.0), motion is unreliable
[20118]         if (depth <= 0.0001 || depth >= 0.9999) {
[20119]             motion = float2(0.0, 0.0);
[20120]         }
[20121]         MotionOutput[pixelCoord] = motion;
[20122]     }

[20157]     // Compute SAD (Sum of Absolute Differences) for a 5x5 block
[20158]     float blockSAD(int2 curCenter, int2 prevCenter, uint2 dims) {
[20159]         float sad = 0.0;
[20160]         [unroll]
[20161]         for (int dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++) {
[20162]             [unroll]
[20163]             for (int dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
[20164]                 int2 cp = clamp(curCenter + int2(dx, dy), int2(0,0), int2(dims) - 1);
[20165]                 int2 pp = clamp(prevCenter + int2(dx, dy), int2(0,0), int2(dims) - 1);
[20166]                 sad += abs(currentGray.Load(int3(cp, 0)) - previousGray.Load(int3(pp, 0)));
[20167]             }
[20168]         }
[20169]         return sad;
[20170]     }
[20171]     [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
[20172]     void main(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
[20173]         uint2 dims = uint2(halfSize.xy);
[20174]         if (id.x >= dims.x || id.y >= dims.y) return;
[20175]         int2 curPos = int2(id.xy);
[20176]         int2 bestOffset = int2(0, 0);
[20177]         float bestSAD = blockSAD(curPos, curPos, dims);
[20178]         // Diamond search: 4 iterations with decreasing step size
[20179]         // Covers effective range of 
[20180] 15 pixels (8+4+2+1)
[20181]         // 
[20182] 30 pixels at full resolution (enough for 60fps truck speed)
[20183]         int steps[4] = { 8, 4, 2, 1 };
[20184]         [unroll]
[20185]         for (int iter = 0; iter < 4; iter++) {
[20186]             int step = steps[iter];
[20187]             // 8-connected diamond pattern
[20188]             int2 offsets[8] = {
[20189]                 int2( step,     0),
[20190]                 int2(-step,     0),
[20191]                 int2(    0,  step),
[20192]                 int2(    0, -step),
[20193]                 int2( step,  step),
[20194]                 int2(-step,  step),
[20195]                 int2( step, -step),
[20196]                 int2(-step, -step)
[20197]             };
[20198]             [unroll]
[20199]             for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
[20200]                 int2 candidate = bestOffset + offsets[i];
[20201]                 int2 prevPos = curPos + candidate;
[20202]                 // Bounds check
[20203]                 if (prevPos.x < 0 || prevPos.y < 0 ||
[20204]                     prevPos.x >= (int)dims.x || prevPos.y >= (int)dims.y)
[20205]                     continue;
[20206]                 float sad = blockSAD(curPos, prevPos, dims);
[20207]                 if (sad < bestSAD) {
[20208]                     bestSAD = sad;
[20209]                     bestOffset = candidate;
[20210]                 }
[20211]             }
[20212]         }
[20213]         // Output flow in half-res pixel space (will be upscaled to full res)
[20214]         flowOutput[id.xy] = float2(bestOffset);
[20215]     }
[20216]     Texture2D<float4> currentColor  : register(t0);
[20217]     Texture2D<float4> previousColor : register(t1);
[20218]     Texture2D<float2> coarseFlow    : register(t2);

[20228]     // 3x3 block SAD on luminance
[20229]     float blockSAD3(int2 curCenter, int2 prevCenter, uint2 dims) {
[20230]         float sad = 0.0;
[20231]         [unroll]
[20232]         for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
[20233]             [unroll]
[20234]             for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
[20235]                 int2 cp = clamp(curCenter + int2(dx, dy), int2(0,0), int2(dims) - 1);
[20236]                 int2 pp = clamp(prevCenter + int2(dx, dy), int2(0,0), int2(dims) - 1);
[20237]                 float lc = luma(currentColor.Load(int3(cp, 0)).rgb);
[20238]                 float lp = luma(previousColor.Load(int3(pp, 0)).rgb);
[20239]                 sad += abs(lc - lp);
[20240]             }
[20241]         }
[20242]         return sad;
[20243]     }
[20244]     [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
[20245]     void main(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
[20246]         uint2 dims = uint2(fullSize.xy);
[20247]         if (id.x >= dims.x || id.y >= dims.y) return;
[20248]         int2 curPos = int2(id.xy);
[20249]         // Sample coarse flow and upscale (
[20250] 2 because coarse is at half res)
[20251]         int2 halfPos = curPos / 2;
[20252]         halfPos = clamp(halfPos, int2(0,0), int2(halfSize.xy) - 1);
[20253]         float2 coarse = coarseFlow.Load(int3(halfPos, 0)) * 2.0; // Scale to full res
[20254]         // Refine: search 
[20255] 2 around coarse estimate
[20256]         int2 baseOffset = int2(round(coarse));
[20257]         int2 bestOffset = baseOffset;
[20258]         float bestSAD = blockSAD3(curPos, curPos + baseOffset, dims);
[20259]         [unroll]
[20260]         for (int dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++) {
[20261]             [unroll]
[20262]             for (int dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) {
[20263]                 if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
[20264]                 int2 candidate = baseOffset + int2(dx, dy);
[20265]                 int2 prevPos = curPos + candidate;
[20266]                 if (prevPos.x < 0 || prevPos.y < 0 ||
[20267]                     prevPos.x >= (int)dims.x || prevPos.y >= (int)dims.y)
[20268]                     continue;
[20269]                 float sad = blockSAD3(curPos, prevPos, dims);
[20270]                 if (sad < bestSAD) {
[20271]                     bestSAD = sad;
[20272]                     bestOffset = candidate;
[20273]                 }
[20274]             }
[20275]         }
[20276]         // Confidence: if best SAD is still high, the flow is unreliable
[20277]         // SAD per pixel: 9 pixels in 3x3, max diff ~1.0 each 
[20278]  max SAD ~9.0
[20279]         // Good match: SAD < 0.3, poor match: SAD > 1.5
[20280]         float confidence = saturate(1.0 - bestSAD / 1.5);
[20281]         // Scale flow by confidence 
[20282]  unreliable flow fades to zero
[20283]         float2 flow = float2(bestOffset) * confidence;
[20284]         refinedFlow[id.xy] = flow;
[20285]     }
[20286]     Texture2D<float2> opticalFlow    : register(t0);  // Our computed flow (pixel space)
[20287]     Texture2D<float2> depthReproMV   : register(t1);  // Depth-reprojection MVs (pixel space)
[20288]     RWTexture2D<float2> mergedOutput : register(u0);
[20289]     cbuffer FlowConstants : register(b0) {
[20290]         float4 fullSize;
[20291]         float4 halfSize;
[20292]         float4 params;      // {unused, unused, confidenceThreshold, unused}
[20293]     };
[20294]     [numthreads(8, 8, 1)]
[20295]     void main(uint3 id : SV_DispatchThreadID) {
[20296]         uint2 dims = uint2(fullSize.xy);
[20297]         if (id.x >= dims.x || id.y >= dims.y) return;
[20298]         float2 oflow  = opticalFlow.Load(int3(id.xy, 0));
[20299]         float2 drepro = depthReproMV.Load(int3(id.xy, 0));
[20300]         // Compute difference between optical flow and depth reprojection
[20301]         float2 diff = oflow - drepro;
[20302]         float diffLen = length(diff);
[20303]         // If flows agree (diff < 2 pixels), trust depth reprojection (it's more precise for static geo)
[20304]         // If they disagree, blend towards optical flow (object is moving independently)
[20305]         // Smooth transition: fully depth-repro at diff=0, fully optical at diff>=6 pixels
[20306]         float blendFactor = saturate((diffLen - 2.0) / 4.0);
[20307]         // Optical flow magnitude: if flow is very small (< 0.5 px), it's likely noise
[20308]         float oflowLen = length(oflow);
[20309]         float oflowValid = step(0.5, oflowLen);
[20310]         // Final blend: use optical flow where it shows significant independent motion
[20311]         float2 merged = lerp(drepro, oflow, blendFactor * oflowValid);
[20312]         // 3x3 median-like filter: average with neighbors to reduce noise
[20313]         // (Only apply to optical flow contribution to preserve depth repro precision)
[20314]         if (blendFactor > 0.1) {
[20315]             float2 sum = merged;
[20316]             float count = 1.0;
[20317]             [unroll]
[20318]             for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
[20319]                 [unroll]
[20320]                 for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
[20321]                     if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
[20322]                     int2 npos = clamp(int2(id.xy) + int2(dx, dy), int2(0,0), int2(dims) - 1);
[20323]                     float2 nof = opticalFlow.Load(int3(npos, 0));
[20324]                     float2 ndr = depthReproMV.Load(int3(npos, 0));
[20325]                     float nd = length(nof - ndr);
[20326]                     float nb = saturate((nd - 2.0) / 4.0);
[20327]                     float nv = step(0.5, length(nof));
[20328]                     sum += lerp(ndr, nof, nb * nv);
[20329]                     count += 1.0;
[20330]                 }
[20331]             }
[20332]             merged = sum / count;
[20333]         }
[20334]         mergedOutput[id.xy] = merged;
[20335]     }

[20651] // --- Utility functions ---
[20652] // Sobel-based normal generation from height (luminance of texture)
[20653] // Uses ddx/ddy for correct texel step at any resolution/mip level.
[20654] // normalDetailScale controls the kernel spread (1.0=one texel, 2.0=two texels, etc.)
[20655] float3 calcNormalFromHeight(Texture2D<float4> tex, SamplerState samp, float2 uv, float intensity) {
[20656]     // Compute texel step from screen-space derivatives of UV
[20657]     float2 dx = ddx(uv) * normalDetailScale;
[20658]     float2 dy = ddy(uv) * normalDetailScale;
[20659]     // 3x3 Sobel kernel sampling (luminance-based height)
[20660]     float tl = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv - dx - dy, mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20661]     float  t = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv      - dy, mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20662]     float tr = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv + dx - dy, mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20663]     float  l = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv - dx,      mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20664]     float  r = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv + dx,      mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20665]     float bl = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv - dx + dy, mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20666]     float  b = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv      + dy, mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20667]     float br = dot(tex.SampleBias(samp, uv + dx + dy, mipBias).rgb, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20668]     float dX = (tr + 2.0*r + br) - (tl + 2.0*l + bl);
[20669]     float dY = (bl + 2.0*b + br) - (tl + 2.0*t + tr);
[20670]     return normalize(float3(-dX * intensity, -dY * intensity, 1.0));
[20671] // Adjust saturation around the luminance
[20672] float3 adjustSaturation(float3 color, float sat) {
[20673]     float lum = dot(color, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20674]     return lerp(float3(lum, lum, lum), color, sat);
[20675] // Contrast around midpoint 0.5
[20676] float3 adjustContrast(float3 color, float contrast) {
[20677]     return (color - 0.5) * contrast + 0.5;
[20678] // --- Cotangent-frame TBN for normal-mapped road shaders ---
[20679] // Reconstructed from ETS2 DXBC: builds tangent/bitangent from
[20680] // cross(geometricNormal, screenSpacePositionDerivatives) + UV gradients.
[20681] // geoN = TEXCOORD1 (geometric normal), worldP = TEXCOORD2 (position data)
[20682] void computeCotangentTBN(float3 geoN, float3 worldP, float2 uv,
[20683]                           out float3 T, out float3 B) {
[20684]     float3 dPdx = ddx(worldP);
[20685]     float3 dPdy = ddy(worldP);
[20686]     // Cross products with geometric normal 
[20687]  tangent-plane basis
[20688]     float3 crossN_dPdx = cross(geoN, dPdx);
[20689]     float3 crossN_dPdy = cross(geoN, dPdy);
[20690]     // UV derivatives (V is flipped in the engine: 1-uv.y)
[20691]     float dVdx = -ddx(uv.y);
[20692]     float dVdy = -ddy(uv.y);
[20693]     float dUdx = ddx(uv.x);
[20694]     float dUdy = ddy(uv.x);
[20695]     T = normalize(crossN_dPdy * dVdx - crossN_dPdx * dVdy);
[20696]     B = normalize(crossN_dPdy * dUdx - crossN_dPdx * dUdy);
[20697] // Unpack normal map, transform to world space via cotangent frame,
[20698] // compute nmFactor for albedo self-shadowing.
[20699] // Convention: worldNormal = B*nm.x + T*nm.y + geoN*nm.z (matches ETS2 DXBC)
[20700] float3 transformNormalMap(Texture2D<float4> nmTex, SamplerState nmSamp,
[20701]                           float2 uv, float3 T, float3 B, float3 geoN,
[20702]                           out float nmFactor) {
[20703]     float2 nmXY = nmTex.SampleBias(nmSamp, uv, mipBias).xy;
[20704]     nmXY = nmXY * 2.007812 - 1.007812; // exact DXBC unpack constants
[20705]     float nmZ = sqrt(max(1.0 - dot(nmXY, nmXY), 0.0));
[20706]     // World normal (DXBC convention: B
[20707] nx + T
[20708] ny + N
[20709]     float3 nmWorld = B * nmXY.x + T * nmXY.y + geoN * nmZ;
[20710]     // Self-shadowing factor (from DXBC: mad + min clamp)
[20711]     nmFactor = min(nmZ * 0.399902 + 0.600098, 1.0);
[20712]     return normalize(nmWorld);
[20713] // CB11: injected by mod 
[20714]  row2.w = vegetation thickness multiplier
[20715] cbuffer VegCB9 : register(b11) {
[20716]     float4 vegCB9_row0; // sun/light direction
[20717]     float4 vegCB9_row1; // view normal
[20718]     float4 vegCB9_row2; // .xyz=ambient, .w=vegThickness
[20719] #define vegThickness vegCB9_row2.w
[20720] cbuffer VegConfig : register(b10) {
[20721]     // Row 1
[20722]     float vegAlphaPower;
[20723]     float vegNormalIntensity;
[20724]     float vegMipBias;
[20725]     float vegMipDensity;        // legacy logistic boost (kept for compat)
[20726]     // Row 2
[20727]     float vegSaturation;
[20728]     float vegBrightness;
[20729]     float vegContrast;
[20730]     float vegSharpening;
[20731]     // Row 3
[20732]     float vegSpecularMul;
[20733]     float vegRoughnessMul;
[20734]     float vegSSSIntensity;
[20735]     float vegEdgeBoost;
[20736]     // Row 4: structural density
[20737]     float vegAlphaMipSharpness; // mip levels sharper for alpha SampleGrad
[20738]     float vegAlphaRescale;      // coverage rescaling strength at distance
[20739]     float vegAlphaHashJitter;   // stochastic threshold noise
[20740]     float vegNormalMipBoost;    // extra normal intensity at distance
[20741] // ============================================================================
[20742] // Mip Level Estimation
[20743] // Uses UV derivatives to approximate which mip the GPU would pick.
[20744] // ============================================================================
[20745] float vegEstimateMip(float2 uv, float2 texSize) {
[20746]     float2 dxUV = ddx(uv) * texSize;
[20747]     float2 dyUV = ddy(uv) * texSize;
[20748]     float maxSqLen = max(dot(dxUV, dxUV), dot(dyUV, dyUV));
[20749]     return 0.5 * log2(max(maxSqLen, 1.0));
[20750] // ============================================================================
[20751] // Dual-Mip Alpha Coverage
[20752] // THE CORE STRUCTURAL FIX for sparse distant vegetation.
[20753] // Problem: mipmaps average alpha values, narrowing the distribution.
[20754] // At mip 0, foliage alpha is bimodal (0 or 1 with sharp edges).
[20755] // At mip 3+, alpha clusters around 0.3-0.5 
[20756]  many texels that passed
[20757] // at mip 0 now FAIL the threshold test. Result: sparse trees at distance.
[20758] // Solution (3 parts):
[20759] // 1) DUAL-MIP SAMPLING: Sample alpha from a SHARPER mip level than color.
[20760] //    Color stays at normal mip (no shimmer). Alpha uses SampleGrad with
[20761] //    reduced UV gradients 
[20762]  effectively 1-2 mip levels sharper.
[20763] //    Cost: 1 extra texture fetch (the only extra fetch in this shader).
[20764] // 2) COVERAGE RESCALING: Stretch alpha distribution around the threshold
[20765] //    proportional to mip level. This pushes near-threshold values to
[20766] //    clearly pass or fail, restoring the bimodal distribution of mip 0.
[20767] //    Like the Source Engine / UE approach to alpha coverage preservation.
[20768] // 3) STOCHASTIC THRESHOLD: Spatially-varying test threshold prevents
[20769] //    uniform density bands at LOD transitions. Deterministic noise
[20770] //    (same pattern each frame) ensures temporal stability.
[20771] // Why this is NOT a parametric tweak:
[20772] // - pow(alpha) shifts the ENTIRE curve uniformly 
[20773]  doesn't fix coverage
[20774] // - alpha += k*a*(1-a) is a logistic boost peaking at 0.5 
[20775]  too weak
[20776] // - SampleBias makes color AND alpha sharper 
[20777]  causes shimmer
[20778] // - This approach decouples alpha resolution from color resolution
[20779] // ============================================================================
[20780] float vegDualMipAlpha(
[20781]     Texture2D<float4> tex, SamplerState samp,
[20782]     float2 uv, float baseAlpha, float estimatedMip,
[20783]     float2 screenPos)
[20784]     // mipFactor: 0 at close range (mip < 0.5), 1 at distance (mip >= 3)
[20785]     float mipFactor = saturate((estimatedMip - 0.5) / 2.5);
[20786]     float rawAlpha = baseAlpha;
[20787]     // --- Part 1: Dual-mip alpha sampling ---
[20788]     // Reduce UV gradients 
[20789]  hardware samples alpha at lower mip level.
[20790]     // At close range (mipFactor=0): gradScale=1.0 (identical to normal sample).
[20791]     // At distance (mipFactor=1): gradScale
[20792] 0.35 (~1.5 mips sharper).
[20793]     // SampleGrad preserves anisotropic filtering direction.
[20794]     if (vegAlphaMipSharpness > 0.01) {
[20795]         float2 dxUV = ddx(uv);
[20796]         float2 dyUV = ddy(uv);
[20797]         float gradScale = exp2(-vegAlphaMipSharpness * mipFactor);
[20798]         float sharpAlpha = tex.SampleGrad(samp, uv, dxUV * gradScale, dyUV * gradScale).w;
[20799]         // Smooth blend: original at close range, sharp at distance
[20800]         rawAlpha = lerp(baseAlpha, sharpAlpha, mipFactor);
[20801]     }
[20802]     // --- Part 2: Coverage rescaling ---
[20803]     // Stretch alpha distribution around threshold to restore mip-0 coverage.
[20804]     // At mip 0: rescale=1.0 (no change). At mip 3+: rescale=alphaRescale.
[20805]     // Example with rescale=3.0, threshold=0.05:
[20806]     //   alpha=0.10 
[20807]  0.20 (clearly passes)
[20808]     //   alpha=0.30 
[20809]  0.80 (strong pass)
[20810]     //   alpha=0.03 
[20811]  0.00 (clearly fails)
[20812]     // This makes the alpha decision MORE binary at distance = more coverage.
[20813]     if (vegAlphaRescale > 1.01) {
[20814]         float threshold = 0.049988;
[20815]         float rescale = lerp(1.0, vegAlphaRescale, mipFactor);
[20816]         rawAlpha = saturate((rawAlpha - threshold) * rescale + threshold);
[20817]     }
[20818]     // --- Legacy logistic boost (backward compat, usually 0) ---
[20819]     if (vegMipDensity > 0.001) {
[20820]         float mipComp = vegMipDensity * mipFactor;
[20821]         rawAlpha = saturate(rawAlpha + mipComp * rawAlpha * (1.0 - rawAlpha) * 4.0);
[20822]     }
[20823]     return rawAlpha;
[20824] // ============================================================================
[20825] // Stochastic alpha test threshold
[20826] // Adds deterministic spatial noise to the discard threshold.
[20827] // Prevents uniform density bands at LOD boundaries.
[20828] // The noise is per-2x2 pixel block for temporal stability.
[20829] // ============================================================================
[20830] float vegStochasticThreshold(float2 screenPos, float estimatedMip) {
[20831]     if (vegAlphaHashJitter < 0.001) return 0.0;
[20832]     float mipFactor = saturate((estimatedMip - 0.5) / 2.5);
[20833]     // Deterministic hash: stable across frames, varies spatially
[20834]     float2 seed = floor(screenPos * 0.5); // 2x2 block coherence
[20835]     float noise = frac(sin(dot(seed, float2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453);
[20836]     return (noise - 0.5) * vegAlphaHashJitter * mipFactor;
[20837] // ============================================================================
[20838] // Unsharp Mask 
[20839]  Laplacian-based bidirectional sharpening.
[20840] // Uses second screen-space derivatives (approximated Laplacian).
[20841] // Subtracting the Laplacian BRIGHTENS bright peaks AND DARKENS dark valleys.
[20842] // This PRESERVES shadow depth, unlike one-sided multiplicative brightening.
[20843] // ============================================================================
[20844] float3 vegUnsharpMask(float3 color, float amount) {
[20845]     float3 laplacian = ddx(ddx(color)) + ddy(ddy(color));
[20846]     return clamp(color - amount * laplacian, 0.0, 1.0);
[20847] // ============================================================================
[20848] // Micro-Detail Normal from Screen Derivatives
[20849] // FREE: uses ddx/ddy of already-sampled luminance.
[20850] // Produces sub-texel normal variation that improves light response.
[20851] // Enhanced: scales with distance to compensate for normal map blur.
[20852] // ============================================================================
[20853] float3 vegMicroNormal(float3 baseN, float3 color, float intensity, float estimatedMip) {
[20854]     float lum = dot(color, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20855]     float lumDx = ddx(lum);
[20856]     float lumDy = ddy(lum);
[20857]     // At distance, normal maps lose detail from mipmapping.
[20858]     // Boost the screen-space micro-normal to compensate.
[20859]     float mipFactor = saturate((estimatedMip - 0.5) / 2.5);
[20860]     float effectiveIntensity = intensity * (1.0 + vegNormalMipBoost * mipFactor);
[20861]     float3 perturbed = normalize(float3(
[20862]         baseN.x - lumDx * effectiveIntensity,
[20863]         baseN.y - lumDy * effectiveIntensity,
[20864]         baseN.z
[20865]     ));
[20866]     return perturbed;
[20867] // ============================================================================
[20868] // Adaptive Saturation 
[20869]  luminance-aware
[20870] // ============================================================================
[20871] float3 vegAdaptiveSaturation(float3 color, float sat) {
[20872]     float lum = dot(color, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[20873]     float adaptiveSat = lerp(1.0, sat, saturate(lum * 2.0));
[20874]     return lerp(float3(lum, lum, lum), color, adaptiveSat);
[20875] float3 vegAdjustContrast(float3 color, float contrast) {
[20876]     return (color - 0.5) * contrast + 0.5;
[20877] // ============================================================================
[20878] // SSS Factor (energy-conserving, AO-modulated)
[20879] // ============================================================================
[20880] float vegComputeSSSFactor(float3 N, float3 lightDir, float intensity, float ao) {
[20881]     float NdotL_back = saturate(dot(-N, lightDir));
[20882]     float sss = pow(NdotL_back, 2.0) * intensity;
[20883]     return sss * ao;
[20884] // ============================================================================
[20885] // Edge Factor (multiplicative, capped)
[20886] // ============================================================================
[20887] float vegComputeEdgeFactor(float3 N, float3 viewDir, float boost) {
[20888]     float NdotV = saturate(dot(N, viewDir));
[20889]     float rim = pow(1.0 - NdotV, 3.0);
[20890]     return 1.0 + rim * boost;

[20898] Texture2D    tScene : register(t0);

[20915] struct PS_INPUT {
[20916]     float4 pos : SV_Position;
[20917]     float2 uv  : TEXCOORD0;
[20918] float luminance(float3 c) {
[20919]     return dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
[20920] // Simple filmic curve (Unreal-like) 
[20921]  the only active tonemap
[20922] float3 simpleFilmic(float3 x) {
[20923]     x = max(0, x - 0.004);
[20924]     return (x*(6.2*x + 0.5)) / (x*(6.2*x + 1.7) + 0.06);
[20925] float4 main(PS_INPUT input) : SV_Target0 {
[20926]     // Debug override
[20927]     if (debugRed > 0.5) return float4(1, 0, 0, 1);
[20928]     
[20929]     float3 c = tScene.Sample(sPoint, input.uv).rgb;
[20930]     
[20931]     // 1. Brightness (pre-curve exposure)
[20932]     c *= brightness;
[20933]     
[20934]     // 2. Filmic curve (replaces simple gamma)
[20935]     //    Blends between original and filmic based on filmicStrength
[20936]     if (filmicStrength > 0.01) {
[20937]         // Lift to pseudo-HDR range for filmic to work properly
[20938]         // (filmic curves expect >1.0 input for highlight compression)
[20939]         float3 lifted = c * 2.0;  // simulate 2x exposure headroom
[20940]         float3 curved = simpleFilmic(lifted);
[20941]         c = lerp(c, curved, filmicStrength);
[20942]     }
[20943]     
[20944]     // 3. Contrast (S-curve centered at 0.5)
[20945]     if (abs(contrast - 1.0) > 0.01)
[20946]         c = saturate((c - 0.5) * contrast + 0.5);
[20947]     
[20948]     // 4. Shadow lift / Highlight gain (luminance-dependent)
[20949]     float lum = luminance(c);
[20950]     float shadowMask    = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.4, lum);
[20951]     float highlightMask = smoothstep(0.5, 1.0, lum);
[20952]     
[20953]     c += shadowLift * shadowMask;
[20954]     c *= lerp(1.0, highlightGain, highlightMask);
[20955]     
[20956]     // 5. Color temperature (warmth)
[20957]     if (warmth > 0.0) {
[20958]         c.r *= 1.0 + warmth * 0.15;
[20959]         c.b *= 1.0 - warmth * 0.10;
[20960]     } else {
[20961]         c.r *= 1.0 + warmth * 0.10;
[20962]         c.b *= 1.0 - warmth * 0.15;
[20963]     }
[20964]     
[20965]     // 6. Tint (per-channel multiply)
[20966]     c *= float3(tintR, tintG, tintB);
[20967]     
[20968]     // 7. Saturation
[20969]     float gray = luminance(c);
[20970]     c = lerp(float3(gray, gray, gray), c, saturation);
[20971]     
[20972]     return float4(saturate(c), 1.0);
[20973] Texture2D tex : register(t0);
