[20983] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[20984]     float4 c = tex.Load(int3(pos.xy, 0));
[20985]     // Per-channel color boost
[20986]     c.r *= boostR;
[20987]     c.g *= boostG;
[20988]     c.b *= boostB;
[20989]     // Brightness (simple multiply)
[20990]     c.rgb *= brightness;
[20991]     // Contrast (pivot around 0.5)
[20992]     c.rgb = (c.rgb - 0.5) * contrast + 0.5;
[20993]     // Saturation (luminance-preserving)
[20994]     float lum = dot(c.rgb, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
[20995]     c.rgb = lerp(float3(lum, lum, lum), c.rgb, saturation);
[20996]     return c;
[20997] Texture2D tex : register(t0);
[20998] cbuffer Params : register(b0) {
[20999]     float normalIntensity;
[21000]     float3 pad;
[21001] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21002]     float4 n = tex.Load(int3(pos.xy, 0));
[21003]     // Scale XY deviation from center
[21004]     n.xy = (n.xy - 0.5) * normalIntensity + 0.5;
[21005]     // Recompute Z from unit-sphere constraint
[21006]     float2 dec = n.xy * 2.0 - 1.0;
[21007]     float  zSq = 1.0 - dot(dec, dec);
[21008]     n.z = sqrt(max(zSq, 0.0)) * 0.5 + 0.5;
[21009]     return n;
[21010]     Texture2D<float4> srcTex : register(t0);
[21011]     cbuffer CasClarityParams : register(b0) {
[21012]         float casSharpness;     // 0=none, 1=max
[21013]         float clarityStrength;  // 0=none, 1=strong
[21014]         float rcpWidth;
[21015]         float rcpHeight;
[21016]         float casEnabled;       // 0 or 1
[21017]         float clarityEnabled;   // 0 or 1
[21018]         float _pad0;
[21019]         float _pad1;
[21020]     };
[21021]     float getLuma(float3 c) {
[21022]         return dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));
[21023]     }
[21024]     // Clarity: 13-tap diamond kernel local contrast enhancement (luma-based)
[21025]     float3 applyClarity(int2 px, float3 center) {
[21026]         // Ring 1 (distance 1)
[21027]         float3 n1 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0,-1), 0)).rgb;
[21028]         float3 s1 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0, 1), 0)).rgb;
[21029]         float3 w1 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2(-1, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21030]         float3 e1 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 1, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21031]         // Ring 2 (distance 2)
[21032]         float3 n2 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0,-2), 0)).rgb;
[21033]         float3 s2 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0, 2), 0)).rgb;
[21034]         float3 w2 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2(-2, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21035]         float3 e2 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 2, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21036]         // Ring 3 (distance 3)
[21037]         float3 n3 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0,-3), 0)).rgb;
[21038]         float3 s3 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0, 3), 0)).rgb;
[21039]         float3 w3 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2(-3, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21040]         float3 e3 = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 3, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21041]         // Weighted local average (total weight = 4*4 + 4*2 + 4*1 = 28)
[21042]         float3 localAvg = (n1 + s1 + w1 + e1) * 4.0
[21043]                         + (n2 + s2 + w2 + e2) * 2.0
[21044]                         + (n3 + s3 + w3 + e3) * 1.0;
[21045]         localAvg /= 28.0;
[21046]         float centerLuma = getLuma(center);
[21047]         float avgLuma    = getLuma(localAvg);
[21048]         float detail     = centerLuma - avgLuma;
[21049]         // Soft-limit to avoid halos
[21050]         float limited = detail / (1.0 + abs(detail) * 4.0);
[21051]         // Apply with 2.5x multiplier for meaningful slider range
[21052]         float boost = limited * clarityStrength * 2.5;
[21053]         return saturate(center + boost);
[21054]     }
[21055]     // AMD RCAS: noise-aware 5-tap cross sharpening
[21056]     float3 applyCAS(int2 px, float3 center) {
[21057]         float3 b = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0,-1), 0)).rgb;
[21058]         float3 d = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2(-1, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21059]         float3 e = center;
[21060]         float3 f = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 1, 0), 0)).rgb;
[21061]         float3 h = srcTex.Load(int3(px + int2( 0, 1), 0)).rgb;
[21062]         // Perceptual luma
[21063]         float bL = b.g * 0.5 + (b.r + b.b) * 0.25;
[21064]         float dL = d.g * 0.5 + (d.r + d.b) * 0.25;
[21065]         float eL = e.g * 0.5 + (e.r + e.b) * 0.25;
[21066]         float fL = f.g * 0.5 + (f.r + f.b) * 0.25;
[21067]         float hL = h.g * 0.5 + (h.r + h.b) * 0.25;
[21068]         // Noise estimation
[21069]         float nz = 0.25 * (bL + dL + fL + hL) - eL;
[21070]         float range = max(max(bL, dL), max(eL, max(fL, hL)))
[21071]                     - min(min(bL, dL), min(eL, min(fL, hL)));
[21072]         nz = saturate(abs(nz) / (range + 0.001));
[21073]         nz = -0.5 * nz + 1.0;
[21074]         // Adaptive weight per channel
[21075]         float3 mn4 = min(min(b, d), min(f, h));
[21076]         float3 mx4 = max(max(b, d), max(f, h));
[21077]         float3 peakC = float3(1.0, 1.0, 1.0) - mx4;
[21078]         float3 hitMin = mn4 / (4.0 * mx4 + 0.001);
[21079]         float3 hitMax = peakC / (4.0 * (1.001 - mn4));
[21080]         float3 lobeRGB = max(-hitMin, -hitMax);
[21081]         float peak = -(casSharpness * 0.25);
[21082]         float lobe = max(peak, min(max(lobeRGB.r, max(lobeRGB.g, lobeRGB.b)), 0.0)) * nz;
[21083]         float3 result = (b * lobe + d * lobe + f * lobe + h * lobe + e) / (1.0 + 4.0 * lobe);
[21084]         return saturate(result);
[21085]     }
[21086]     float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target {
[21087]         int2 px = int2(pos.xy);
[21088]         float3 color = srcTex.Load(int3(px, 0)).rgb;
[21089]         // Clarity first (recovers texture detail)
[21090]         if (clarityEnabled > 0.5)
[21091]             color = applyClarity(px, color);
[21092]         // CAS second (sharpens edges)
[21093]         if (casEnabled > 0.5)
[21094]             color = applyCAS(px, color);
[21095]         return float4(color, 1.0);
[21096]     }
[21097] saturation
[21098] cbuffer TAACB : register(b0) {
[21099]     float2 texelSize;
[21100]     float2 jitter;
[21101]     float2 resolution;
[21102]     float  feedbackMin;   // history weight (0.88 default)
[21103]     float  clipScale;     // variance clip aggressiveness (1.0 default)
[21104]     float  lumaDiffScale; // luma anti-ghost sensitivity (2.0 default)
[21105]     float  velScale;      // velocity->blend sensitivity (35 default)
[21106]     float  _pad0;
[21107]     float  _pad1;
[21108] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s0);
[21109] SamplerState sPointClamp  : register(s1);
[21110] Texture2D<float4> texColor    : register(t0);
[21111] Texture2D<float4> texVelocity : register(t1);
[21112] Texture2D<float>  texDepth    : register(t2);
[21113] Texture2D<float4> texHistory  : register(t3);
[21114] struct PSOutput {
[21115]     float4 color : SV_Target0;
[21116]     float4 mask  : SV_Target1;
[21117] // ---- Color space conversion: RGB <-> YCoCg ----
[21118] float3 RGBToYCoCg(float3 rgb) {
[21119]     float Y  = dot(rgb, float3(0.25, 0.5, 0.25));
[21120]     float Co = dot(rgb, float3(0.5, 0.0, -0.5));
[21121]     float Cg = dot(rgb, float3(-0.25, 0.5, -0.25));
[21122]     return float3(Y, Co, Cg);
[21123] float3 YCoCgToRGB(float3 ycocg) {
[21124]     float Y  = ycocg.x;
[21125]     float Co = ycocg.y;
[21126]     float Cg = ycocg.z;
[21127]     return float3(Y + Co - Cg, Y + Cg, Y - Co - Cg);
[21128] // ---- Karis anti-flicker weight ----
[21129] float karisWeight(float3 c) {
[21130]     return 1.0 / (1.0 + dot(c, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114)));
[21131] // ---- Catmull-Rom bicubic filter (4x4 taps, 9 bilinear samples) ----
[21132] // Matches PS_004's history sampling for sharp sub-pixel reprojection
[21133] float3 sampleHistoryCatmullRom(Texture2D<float4> tex, SamplerState samp, float2 uv, float2 texSize) {
[21134]     float2 samplePos = uv * texSize;
[21135]     float2 texPos1 = floor(samplePos - 0.5) + 0.5;
[21136]     float2 f = samplePos - texPos1;
[21137]     // Catmull-Rom weights
[21138]     float2 w0 = f * (-0.5 + f * (1.0 - 0.5 * f));
[21139]     float2 w1 = 1.0 + f * f * (-2.5 + 1.5 * f);
[21140]     float2 w2 = f * (0.5 + f * (2.0 - 1.5 * f));
[21141]     float2 w3 = f * f * (-0.5 + 0.5 * f);
[21142]     // Collapse to 2x2 bilinear fetches (Sigg & Hadwiger optimization)
[21143]     float2 w12 = w1 + w2;
[21144]     float2 offset12 = w2 / w12;
[21145]     float2 texPos0  = (texPos1 - 1.0) / texSize;
[21146]     float2 texPos3  = (texPos1 + 2.0) / texSize;
[21147]     float2 texPos12 = (texPos1 + offset12) / texSize;
[21148]     float3 result = 0;
[21149]     // Row 0
[21150]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos0.x,  texPos0.y),  0).rgb * w0.x * w0.y;
[21151]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos12.x, texPos0.y),  0).rgb * w12.x * w0.y;
[21152]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos3.x,  texPos0.y),  0).rgb * w3.x * w0.y;
[21153]     // Row 1+2 (merged via bilinear)
[21154]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos0.x,  texPos12.y), 0).rgb * w0.x * w12.y;
[21155]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos12.x, texPos12.y), 0).rgb * w12.x * w12.y;
[21156]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos3.x,  texPos12.y), 0).rgb * w3.x * w12.y;
[21157]     // Row 3
[21158]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos0.x,  texPos3.y),  0).rgb * w0.x * w3.y;
[21159]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos12.x, texPos3.y),  0).rgb * w12.x * w3.y;
[21160]     result += tex.SampleLevel(samp, float2(texPos3.x,  texPos3.y),  0).rgb * w3.x * w3.y;
[21161]     return max(result, 0.0);
[21162] PSOutput main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) {
[21163]     PSOutput output;
[21164]     // ---- Current color (unjittered center) ----
[21165]     float3 centerColor = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0).rgb;
[21166]     // ---- Closest-depth velocity selection + depth-edge detection (3x3) ----
[21167]     // Matches PS_004: pick velocity from the nearest (closest to camera) pixel.
[21168]     // Also measures depth discontinuity for edge-aware ghosting reduction.
[21169]     float centerDepth = texDepth.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0).x;
[21170]     float closestDepth = centerDepth;
[21171]     float2 closestUV = uv;
[21172]     float maxDepthDiff = 0.0;
[21173]     [unroll]
[21174]     for (int cy = -1; cy <= 1; cy++) {
[21175]         [unroll]
[21176]         for (int cx = -1; cx <= 1; cx++) {
[21177]             float2 sampleUV = uv + float2(cx, cy) * texelSize;
[21178]             float d = texDepth.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, sampleUV, 0).x;
[21179]             maxDepthDiff = max(maxDepthDiff, abs(d - centerDepth));
[21180]             // ETS2 uses standard depth (0=near, 1=far): closest = smallest
[21181]             if (d < closestDepth) {
[21182]                 closestDepth = d;
[21183]                 closestUV = sampleUV;
[21184]             }
[21185]         }
[21186]     }
[21187]     // ---- Velocity from closest-depth pixel (pixel space -> UV space) ----
[21188]     float2 velocity = texVelocity.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, closestUV, 0).xy;
[21189]     velocity *= texelSize;  // pixel -> UV
[21190]     float velMag = length(velocity * resolution);  // magnitude in pixels
[21191]     // ---- 3x3 neighborhood statistics in YCoCg ----
[21192]     float3 centerYCoCg = RGBToYCoCg(centerColor);
[21193]     float  centerWeight = karisWeight(centerColor);
[21194]     float3 nMin = centerYCoCg;
[21195]     float3 nMax = centerYCoCg;
[21196]     float3 wSum = centerYCoCg * centerWeight;
[21197]     float3 wSumSq = centerYCoCg * centerYCoCg * centerWeight;
[21198]     float  totalWeight = centerWeight;
[21199]     // Plus pattern (cross) 
[21200]  more stable than full 3x3 for variance
[21201]     static const int2 offsets[8] = {
[21202]         int2(-1, -1), int2(0, -1), int2(1, -1),
[21203]         int2(-1,  0),              int2(1,  0),
[21204]         int2(-1,  1), int2(0,  1), int2(1,  1)
[21205]     };
[21206]     [unroll]
[21207]     for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
[21208]         float2 sampleUV = uv + float2(offsets[i]) * texelSize;
[21209]         float3 c = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, sampleUV, 0).rgb;
[21210]         float3 ycocg = RGBToYCoCg(c);
[21211]         float w = karisWeight(c);
[21212]         nMin = min(nMin, ycocg);
[21213]         nMax = max(nMax, ycocg);
[21214]         wSum += ycocg * w;
[21215]         wSumSq += ycocg * ycocg * w;
[21216]         totalWeight += w;
[21217]     }
[21218]     float3 mean = wSum / totalWeight;
[21219]     float3 moment = wSumSq / totalWeight;
[21220]     float3 stddev = sqrt(max(0.0, moment - mean * mean));
[21221]     // Tight AABB: intersection of min/max box and variance box
[21222]     float3 aabbMin = max(nMin, mean - stddev * clipScale);
[21223]     float3 aabbMax = min(nMax, mean + stddev * clipScale);
[21224]     // ---- Reproject ----
[21225]     float2 historyUV = uv - velocity;
[21226]     // ---- History sample: Catmull-Rom bicubic (matches PS_004) ----
[21227]     float3 historyColor = sampleHistoryCatmullRom(texHistory, sLinearClamp, historyUV, resolution);
[21228]     // ---- Variance clipping in YCoCg space ----
[21229]     float3 historyYCoCg = RGBToYCoCg(historyColor);
[21230]     // Clip towards center of AABB (Salvi's method)
[21231]     float3 clipCenter = (aabbMin + aabbMax) * 0.5;
[21232]     float3 clipExtent = (aabbMax - aabbMin) * 0.5 + 0.0001;
[21233]     float3 offset = historyYCoCg - clipCenter;
[21234]     float3 ts = abs(clipExtent / max(abs(offset), 0.0001));
[21235]     float  t  = saturate(min(ts.x, min(ts.y, ts.z)));
[21236]     historyYCoCg = clipCenter + offset * t;
[21237]     historyColor = YCoCgToRGB(historyYCoCg);
[21238]     historyColor = max(historyColor, 0.0);
[21239]     // ---- Luminance-based anti-ghosting ----
[21240]     float lumaCenter  = dot(centerColor, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[21241]     float lumaHistory = dot(historyColor, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
[21242]     float lumaDiff = abs(lumaCenter - lumaHistory) / max(lumaCenter, 0.01);
[21243]     // ---- Blend factor (matches PS_004 formula exactly) ----
[21244]     // scaledVel = min(velMag * velScale, 0.5)
[21245]     // confidence = pow(1.0 - scaledVel, 1.5)
[21246]     // blendFactor = 1.0 - (1.0 - baseBlend) * confidence
[21247]     float scaledVel = min(velMag * velScale, 0.5);
[21248]     float confidence = pow(1.0 - scaledVel, 1.5);
[21249]     // Reduce history weight when luminance diverges (ghosting detection)
[21250]     confidence *= saturate(1.0 - lumaDiff * lumaDiffScale);
[21251]     // Depth-edge: reduce history at object boundaries where ghosting is most visible
[21252]     // Only activate if depth buffer is valid (non-zero center depth)
[21253]     if (centerDepth > 0.0001) {
[21254]         float edgeFactor = smoothstep(0.002, 0.03, maxDepthDiff);
[21255]         confidence *= (1.0 - edgeFactor * 0.6);
[21256]     }
[21257]     float baseBlend = 1.0 - feedbackMin;
[21258]     float blendFactor = 1.0 - (1.0 - baseBlend) * confidence;
[21259]     blendFactor = clamp(blendFactor, baseBlend, 1.0);
[21260]     // First frame / out-of-bounds: use current
[21261]     if (any(historyUV < 0.0) || any(historyUV > 1.0))
[21262]         blendFactor = 1.0;
[21263]     float historyLum = dot(historyColor, float3(1, 1, 1));
[21264]     if (historyLum < 0.001) blendFactor = 1.0;
[21265]     // ---- Final blend with Karis weighting for anti-flicker ----
[21266]     float wCurrent = karisWeight(centerColor);
[21267]     float wHistory = karisWeight(historyColor);
[21268]     float3 resolved = (centerColor * wCurrent * blendFactor +
[21269]                        historyColor * wHistory * (1.0 - blendFactor))
[21270]                      / (wCurrent * blendFactor + wHistory * (1.0 - blendFactor) + 0.0001);
[21271]     output.color = float4(resolved, 1.0);
[21272]     output.mask  = float4(confidence, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
[21273]     return output;
[21274] cbuffer CASCB : register(b0) {
[21275]     float2 texelSize;
[21276]     float  sharpness;
[21277]     float  _pad;
[21278] SamplerState sPointClamp : register(s0);
[21279] Texture2D<float4> texColor : register(t0);
[21280] float getLuma(float3 c) { return dot(c, float3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114)); }
[21281] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21282]     // Sample center + 4 cardinal neighbors
[21283]     float3 c = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0).rgb;
[21284]     float3 n = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv + float2(0, -texelSize.y), 0).rgb;
[21285]     float3 s = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv + float2(0,  texelSize.y), 0).rgb;
[21286]     float3 w = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv + float2(-texelSize.x, 0), 0).rgb;
[21287]     float3 e = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv + float2( texelSize.x, 0), 0).rgb;
[21288]     // Edge-adaptive weight (AMD CAS style)
[21289]     float3 mn = min(c, min(min(n, s), min(w, e)));
[21290]     float3 mx = max(c, max(max(n, s), max(w, e)));
[21291]     float3 amp = min(mn, 1.0 - mx) / max(mx, 0.001);
[21292]     // CAS filter: peak clamped to safe range [-0.125, 0]
[21293]     // sharpness 0..1 maps to peak 0..-0.125
[21294]     float peak = -0.125 * sharpness;
[21295]     float3 wt = clamp(amp * peak, peak, 0.0);
[21296]     float3 casResult = (c + wt * (n + s + w + e)) / (1.0 + 4.0 * wt);
[21297]     // Additional unsharp mask for visible effect
[21298]     // blur = average of neighbors, detail = center - blur
[21299]     float3 blur = (n + s + w + e) * 0.25;
[21300]     float3 detail = c - blur;
[21301]     // Edge-aware: scale detail by local contrast to avoid noise
[21302]     float lumaContrast = abs(getLuma(c) - getLuma(blur));
[21303]     float edgeMask = saturate(lumaContrast * 10.0);  // strong edges 
[21304]  sharpen more
[21305]     // Combine CAS + unsharp mask
[21306]     // sharpness 0..1 
[21307]  unsharp strength 0..1.5
[21308]     float unsharpStr = sharpness * 1.5;
[21309]     float3 result = casResult + detail * unsharpStr * edgeMask;
[21310]     return float4(saturate(result), 1.0);
[21311] cbuffer TonemapCB : register(b0) {
[21312]     float2 texelSize;
[21313]     float  exposure;
[21314]     float  contrast;
[21315]     float  saturation;
[21316]     float  vibrance;
[21317]     float  gamma;
[21318]     float  lutStrength;
[21319]     float3 lift;
[21320]     float  localContrast;   // 0..1.5 
[21321]  luminance unsharp mask strength
[21322]     float3 gain;
[21323]     float  filmicAmount;    // 0..1 
[21324]  blend between linear and ACES curve
[21325]     float  aeTargetLum;
[21326]     float  aeMinEV;
[21327]     float  aeMaxEV;
[21328]     float  aeEnabled;
[21329]     float  vignetteAmount;  // 0..1 
[21330]  corner darkening
[21331]     float  vignetteSoftness;// 0..1 
[21332]  falloff softness
[21333]     float  shadowLift;      // 0..0.1 
[21334]  toe lift (raises blacks)
[21335]     float  highlightSqueeze;// 0..0.5 
[21336]  shoulder compression (rolls off whites)
[21337]     float  lightTemp;       // 0..1 
[21338]  warm highlights / cool shadows (natural outdoor lighting)
[21339]     float  midtoneBoost;    // 0..1 
[21340]  lifts midtones (less gray)
[21341]     float  shadowColor;     // 0..1 
[21342]  blue tint in shadows (skylight simulation)
[21343]     float  sunWarmth;       // 0..1 
[21344]  golden warmth in bright areas (sunlight simulation)
[21345]     float  grainAmount;     // 0..0.1 
[21346]  film grain intensity
[21347]     float  grainSeed;       // per-frame seed for grain animation
[21348]     float  pad0;
[21349]     float  pad1;
[21350] SamplerState sPointClamp  : register(s0);
[21351] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s1);
[21352] Texture2D<float4> texColor      : register(t0);
[21353] Texture2D<float4> texLUT        : register(t1);  // optional 1024x32 strip LUT
[21354] Texture2D<float>  texAdaptedLum : register(t2);  // 1x1 adapted luminance from auto-exposure
[21355] float getLuma(float3 c) { return dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)); }
[21356] // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
[21357] // ACES Hill filmic tonemap (Krzysztof Narkowicz fit 
[21358]  fast, very close to ACES)
[21359] // Smooths highlights (no harsh clipping) and lifts shadows slightly.
[21360] // Operates in linear space.
[21361] // ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
[21362] float3 acesFilmic(float3 x) {
[21363]     const float a = 2.51;
[21364]     const float b = 0.03;
[21365]     const float c = 2.43;
[21366]     const float d = 0.59;
[21367]     const float e = 0.14;
[21368]     return saturate((x * (a * x + b)) / (x * (c * x + d) + e));
[21369] // Apply 3D LUT stored as 1024x32 strip (32 slices of 32x32)
[21370] float3 applyLUT(float3 color, float strength) {
[21371]     if (strength < 0.001) return color;
[21372]     float3 c = saturate(color);
[21373]     float lutSize = 32.0;
[21374]     // Blue channel selects slice
[21375]     float blueSlice = c.b * (lutSize - 1.0);
[21376]     float slice0 = floor(blueSlice);
[21377]     float slice1 = min(slice0 + 1.0, lutSize - 1.0);
[21378]     float blendFrac = blueSlice - slice0;
[21379]     // UV within each 32x32 slice
[21380]     float2 uvInSlice;
[21381]     uvInSlice.x = (c.r * (lutSize - 1.0) + 0.5) / (lutSize * lutSize); // 0..1 across 1024
[21382]     uvInSlice.y = (c.g * (lutSize - 1.0) + 0.5) / lutSize;              // 0..1 across 32
[21383]     // Offset by slice index
[21384]     float2 uv0 = float2(uvInSlice.x + slice0 / lutSize, uvInSlice.y);
[21385]     float2 uv1 = float2(uvInSlice.x + slice1 / lutSize, uvInSlice.y);
[21386]     float3 lut0 = texLUT.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv0, 0).rgb;
[21387]     float3 lut1 = texLUT.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv1, 0).rgb;
[21388]     float3 lutColor = lerp(lut0, lut1, blendFrac);
[21389]     return lerp(color, lutColor, strength);
[21390] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21391]     float3 color = texColor.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0).rgb;
[21392]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21393]     // 1. Exposure  (EV stops, optional auto-exposure offset)
[21394]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21395]     float totalExposure = exposure;
[21396]     if (aeEnabled > 0.5) {
[21397]         float adaptedLum = texAdaptedLum.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, float2(0.5, 0.5), 0);
[21398]         if (adaptedLum > 0.001) {
[21399]             float autoEV = log2(aeTargetLum / adaptedLum);
[21400]             autoEV = clamp(autoEV, aeMinEV, aeMaxEV);
[21401]             totalExposure += autoEV;
[21402]         }
[21403]     }
[21404]     color *= exp2(totalExposure);
[21405]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21406]     // 2. ACES Hill filmic curve  (smooth highlight rolloff)
[21407]     //    Blends with linear so user can disable via filmicAmount=0.
[21408]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21409]     if (filmicAmount > 0.001) {
[21410]         float3 filmicColor = acesFilmic(color);
[21411]         color = lerp(saturate(color), filmicColor, filmicAmount);
[21412]     } else {
[21413]         color = saturate(color);
[21414]     }
[21415]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21416]     // 3. Local contrast  (multi-scale luminance unsharp mask)
[21417]     //    - Fine scale  (~3 px): microdetail in textures, foliage, near surfaces
[21418]     //    - Coarse scale (~9 px): mid-range "pop", separates planes at distance
[21419]     //    Both affect luminance only (chroma preserved via ratio). Total cost:
[21420]     //    8 bilinear taps. Detail clamped to 
[21421] 0.15 to prevent halos.
[21422]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21423]     if (localContrast > 0.001) {
[21424]         float curLuma  = getLuma(color);
[21425]         float expScale = exp2(totalExposure);
[21426]         // Neighbor processing must match center: exposure 
[21427]  ACES blend 
[21428]  saturate
[21429]         // to avoid luminance domain mismatch in detail extraction.
[21430]         // --- Fine scale (3px) ---
[21431]         float2 offFine = texelSize * 3.0;
[21432]         float3 nf0 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-offFine.x, -offFine.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21433]         float3 nf1 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( offFine.x, -offFine.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21434]         float3 nf2 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-offFine.x,  offFine.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21435]         float3 nf3 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( offFine.x,  offFine.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21436]         if (filmicAmount > 0.001) {
[21437]             nf0 = lerp(saturate(nf0), acesFilmic(nf0), filmicAmount);
[21438]             nf1 = lerp(saturate(nf1), acesFilmic(nf1), filmicAmount);
[21439]             nf2 = lerp(saturate(nf2), acesFilmic(nf2), filmicAmount);
[21440]             nf3 = lerp(saturate(nf3), acesFilmic(nf3), filmicAmount);
[21441]         } else {
[21442]             nf0 = saturate(nf0); nf1 = saturate(nf1); nf2 = saturate(nf2); nf3 = saturate(nf3);
[21443]         }
[21444]         float blurFine = (getLuma(nf0) + getLuma(nf1) + getLuma(nf2) + getLuma(nf3)) * 0.25;
[21445]         float detailFine = clamp(curLuma - blurFine, -0.15, 0.15);
[21446]         // --- Coarse scale (9px) ---
[21447]         float2 offCoarse = texelSize * 9.0;
[21448]         float3 nc0 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-offCoarse.x, -offCoarse.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21449]         float3 nc1 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( offCoarse.x, -offCoarse.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21450]         float3 nc2 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-offCoarse.x,  offCoarse.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21451]         float3 nc3 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( offCoarse.x,  offCoarse.y), 0).rgb * expScale;
[21452]         if (filmicAmount > 0.001) {
[21453]             nc0 = lerp(saturate(nc0), acesFilmic(nc0), filmicAmount);
[21454]             nc1 = lerp(saturate(nc1), acesFilmic(nc1), filmicAmount);
[21455]             nc2 = lerp(saturate(nc2), acesFilmic(nc2), filmicAmount);
[21456]             nc3 = lerp(saturate(nc3), acesFilmic(nc3), filmicAmount);
[21457]         } else {
[21458]             nc0 = saturate(nc0); nc1 = saturate(nc1); nc2 = saturate(nc2); nc3 = saturate(nc3);
[21459]         }
[21460]         float blurCoarse = (getLuma(nc0) + getLuma(nc1) + getLuma(nc2) + getLuma(nc3)) * 0.25;
[21461]         float detailCoarse = clamp(curLuma - blurCoarse, -0.20, 0.20);
[21462]         // Combine: coarse gets full weight, fine adds 60% on top
[21463]         float totalDetail = detailCoarse + detailFine * 0.6;
[21464]         float newLuma = max(curLuma + totalDetail * localContrast, 0.001);
[21465]         color *= (newLuma / max(curLuma, 0.001));
[21466]     }
[21467]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21468]     // 4. Luminance-aware Lift/Gain  (split-toning)
[21469]     //    lift  = shadow tint  (applied where luma is dark)
[21470]     //    gain  = highlight tint (applied where luma is bright)
[21471]     //    Smooth transition via luma weight, no hard band.
[21472]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21473]     {
[21474]         float l = getLuma(color);
[21475]         float shadowMask    = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.5, l);   // strong in dark, 0 at midtones+
[21476]         float highlightMask = smoothstep(0.5, 1.0, l);          // 0 at midtones, strong in bright
[21477]         // Apply tints
[21478]         color = color + lift * shadowMask;
[21479]         color = color * lerp(float3(1,1,1), gain, highlightMask);
[21480]         color = saturate(color);
[21481]     }
[21482]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21483]     // 5. S-curve contrast  (around midpoint 0.5)
[21484]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21485]     color = (color - 0.5) * contrast + 0.5;
[21486]     color = saturate(color);
[21487]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21488]     // 5b. Toe lift  (raises pure blacks slightly 
[21489]  gives "mood" / cinematic feel)
[21490]     //     Applies an asymmetric remap that lifts shadows without crushing.
[21491]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21492]     if (shadowLift > 0.001) {
[21493]         // Use luminance for lift mask to avoid per-channel hue shift
[21494]         float liftMask = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, 0.35, getLuma(color));
[21495]         color = color + shadowLift * liftMask;
[21496]         color = saturate(color);
[21497]     }
[21498]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21499]     // 5c. Highlight squeeze  (compresses bright values 
[21500]  prevents harsh whites)
[21501]     //     Acts like a soft shoulder on top of the filmic curve.
[21502]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21503]     if (highlightSqueeze > 0.001) {
[21504]         // For values above 0.7, smoothly compress toward 1.0.
[21505]         float3 hMask = smoothstep(0.7, 1.0, color);
[21506]         // Squeeze: x' = x - amount * mask * (x - 0.85)
[21507]         color = color - highlightSqueeze * hMask * max(color - 0.85, 0.0);
[21508]         color = saturate(color);
[21509]     }
[21510]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21511]     // 6. Saturation
[21512]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21513]     float luma = getLuma(color);
[21514]     color = lerp(float3(luma, luma, luma), color, saturation);
[21515]     color = max(color, 0.0);
[21516]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21517]     // 7. Vibrance 
[21518]  boost saturation of desaturated colors more
[21519]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21520]     if (abs(vibrance) > 0.001) {
[21521]         float3 colorClamped = saturate(color);
[21522]         float  vibrLuma = getLuma(colorClamped);
[21523]         float maxC = max(colorClamped.r, max(colorClamped.g, colorClamped.b));
[21524]         float minC = min(colorClamped.r, min(colorClamped.g, colorClamped.b));
[21525]         float chromaSat = (maxC > 0.001) ? (maxC - minC) / maxC : 0.0;
[21526]         float vibranceFactor = 1.0 + vibrance * (1.0 - chromaSat);
[21527]         color = lerp(float3(vibrLuma, vibrLuma, vibrLuma), colorClamped, vibranceFactor);
[21528]         color = max(color, 0.0);
[21529]     }
[21530]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21531]     // 8. Gamma
[21532]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21533]     color = pow(max(color, 0.0), 1.0 / max(gamma, 0.1));
[21534]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21535]     // 9. LUT (optional)
[21536]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21537]     color = applyLUT(color, lutStrength);
[21538]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21539]     // 10. Natural Lighting Simulation
[21540]     //     Simulates outdoor lighting where shadows receive cool blue skylight
[21541]     //     and highlights receive warm golden sunlight.
[21542]     //     This is the #1 technique that makes a game feel "alive" vs "gray".
[21543]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21544]     // 10a. Luminance-based color temperature (warm highlights / cool shadows)
[21545]     if (lightTemp > 0.001) {
[21546]         float lt = getLuma(color);
[21547]         // Shadow zone: pixels below ~0.30 luminance receive cool (blue) skylight
[21548]         float shadowMask = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.05, 0.35, lt);
[21549]         // Highlight zone: pixels above ~0.50 luminance receive warm (golden) sunlight
[21550]         float highlightMask = smoothstep(0.45, 0.90, lt);
[21551]         // Cool shadows: shift R down, B up (skylight = 6500K+)
[21552]         float3 coolShift = float3(-0.020, 0.005, 0.030) * shadowColor * shadowMask;
[21553]         // Warm highlights: shift R up, G slight up, B down (sunlight = ~5000K)
[21554]         float3 warmShift = float3(0.025, 0.008, -0.020) * sunWarmth * highlightMask;
[21555]         color.rgb += (coolShift + warmShift) * lightTemp;
[21556]         color = saturate(color);
[21557]     }
[21558]     // 10b. Midtone boost 
[21559]  lifts the 0.15-0.85 luminance range
[21560]     //      Makes the image feel brighter and more energetic without blowing highlights.
[21561]     //      Uses a bell curve centered at 0.4 (perceptual midpoint).
[21562]     if (midtoneBoost > 0.001) {
[21563]         float mtLum = getLuma(color);
[21564]         // Gaussian-like bell: peak at 0.40 luminance, width ~0.25
[21565]         float midMask = exp(-((mtLum - 0.40) * (mtLum - 0.40)) / (2.0 * 0.25 * 0.25));
[21566]         // Adaptive saturation boost in midtones
[21567]         float satBoost = 1.0 + midtoneBoost * midMask * 0.15;
[21568]         float midLuma = getLuma(color);
[21569]         color = lerp(float3(midLuma, midLuma, midLuma), color, satBoost);
[21570]         // Proportional luminance lift (preserves hue, avoids white shift)
[21571]         float liftRatio = midtoneBoost * midMask * 0.06;
[21572]         color *= (1.0 + liftRatio / max(getLuma(color), 0.001));
[21573]         color = saturate(color);
[21574]     }
[21575]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21576]     // 11. Vignette  (subtle corner darkening 
[21577]  focuses attention)
[21578]     //     Computed in centered UV space, smooth radial falloff.
[21579]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21580]     if (vignetteAmount > 0.001) {
[21581]         float2 cuv = uv - 0.5;
[21582]         // Aspect-correct radius (assume horizontal-major; for ETS2 always wider than tall)
[21583]         float r2 = dot(cuv, cuv) * 1.6;
[21584]         // Inner radius defined by softness; outside fades darker
[21585]         float inner = lerp(0.15, 0.45, vignetteSoftness);
[21586]         float outer = 0.65;
[21587]         float v = smoothstep(inner, outer, r2);
[21588]         color *= (1.0 - vignetteAmount * v);
[21589]     }
[21590]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21591]     // 12. Film Grain 
[21592]  cinematic noise overlay
[21593]     //     Interleaved gradient noise (Jimenez 2014) animated per-frame.
[21594]     //     Applied in midtones only (preserves clean blacks and whites).
[21595]     // -----------------------------------------------------------------------
[21596]     if (grainAmount > 0.001) {
[21597]         // IGN 
[21598]  high-frequency noise with good spatial distribution
[21599]         float2 grainPos = pos.xy + grainSeed * 131.7;
[21600]         float noise = frac(52.9829189 * frac(dot(grainPos, float2(0.06711056, 0.00583715))));
[21601]         noise = noise * 2.0 - 1.0;  // remap to -1..+1
[21602]         // Midtone mask: strongest at 0.5 luma, fades at blacks/whites
[21603]         float grainLuma = getLuma(color);
[21604]         float grainMask = 1.0 - abs(grainLuma - 0.5) * 2.0;
[21605]         grainMask = max(grainMask, 0.2);  // keep some grain everywhere
[21606]         color += noise * grainAmount * grainMask;
[21607]         color = saturate(color);
[21608]     }
[21609]     return float4(saturate(color), 1.0);
[21610] cbuffer LumCB : register(b0) {
[21611]     float2 texelSize;   // 1/fullWidth, 1/fullHeight
[21612]     float2 _pad;
[21613] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s0);
[21614] Texture2D<float4> texScene : register(t0);
[21615] float getLuma(float3 c) { return dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)); }
[21616] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21617]     // 4-tap box downsample 
[21618]  bilinear gives ~4x4 block coverage per tap
[21619]     float3 c0 = texScene.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-0.5, -0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21620]     float3 c1 = texScene.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( 0.5, -0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21621]     float3 c2 = texScene.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-0.5,  0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21622]     float3 c3 = texScene.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( 0.5,  0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21623]     float lum = (getLuma(c0) + getLuma(c1) + getLuma(c2) + getLuma(c3)) * 0.25;
[21624]     // Center-weighted metering: pixels near center contribute more
[21625]     // This prevents sky/ground extremes from dominating the measurement
[21626]     float2 d = uv - float2(0.5, 0.5);
[21627]     float weight = exp(-3.0 * dot(d, d));  // gaussian falloff from center
[21628]     weight = lerp(0.3, 1.0, weight);       // floor at 0.3 so edges still count
[21629]     return float4(lum * weight, weight, 0, 1);
[21630] cbuffer AdaptCB : register(b0) {
[21631]     float adaptSpeedUp;     // bright
[21632] dark recovery
[21633]     float adaptSpeedDown;   // dark
[21634] bright recovery
[21635]     float deltaTime;        // frame delta in seconds
[21636]     float _pad;
[21637] SamplerState sPointClamp  : register(s0);
[21638] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s1);
[21639] Texture2D<float4> texCurrentLum  : register(t0);  // 256x256 mipmap chain (RG = lum*w, w)
[21640] Texture2D<float> texPrevAdapted : register(t1);    // 1x1 previous adapted
[21641] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21642]     // Read current average luminance from smallest mip (1x1 = mip 8 for 256x256)
[21643]     // R = weighted lum sum, G = weight sum 
[21644]  divide to get center-weighted average
[21645]     float2 lumData = texCurrentLum.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, float2(0.5, 0.5), 8.0).rg;
[21646]     float currentLum = (lumData.y > 0.001) ? (lumData.x / lumData.y) : lumData.x;
[21647]     float prevLum    = texPrevAdapted.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, float2(0.5, 0.5), 0);
[21648]     // Asymmetric adaptation speed
[21649]     float speed = (currentLum > prevLum) ? adaptSpeedUp : adaptSpeedDown;
[21650]     float rate  = 1.0 - exp(-speed * deltaTime);
[21651]     float adaptedLum = lerp(prevLum, currentLum, rate);
[21652]     adaptedLum = clamp(adaptedLum, 0.01, 0.95);
[21653]     return float4(adaptedLum, 0, 0, 1);
[21654] cbuffer SSAOCB : register(b0) {
[21655]     float2 texelSize;
[21656]     float  radius;
[21657]     float  bias;
[21658]     float  intensity;
[21659]     float  nearPlane;
[21660]     float  farPlane;
[21661]     int    sampleCount;
[21662] SamplerState sPointClamp  : register(s0);
[21663] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s1);
[21664] Texture2D<float>  texDepth : register(t0);
[21665] // Hash-based noise (per-pixel pseudo-random rotation)
[21666] float2 noise(float2 coord) {
[21667]     float noiseX = frac(sin(dot(coord, float2(12.9898, 78.233))) * 43758.5453);
[21668]     float noiseY = frac(sin(dot(coord, float2(93.9898, 67.345))) * 24634.6345);
[21669]     return float2(noiseX, noiseY) * 2.0 - 1.0;
[21670] // Linearize reversed-Z depth (near=1, far=0)
[21671] float linearizeDepth(float d) {
[21672]     // Reversed-Z: d=1 near, d=0 far
[21673]     // Linear = near * far / (far + d * (near - far))
[21674]     return nearPlane * farPlane / (farPlane + d * (nearPlane - farPlane));
[21675] // Sample kernel 
[21676]  Alchemy SSAO style (simplified)
[21677] static const float3 kernel[16] = {
[21678]     float3( 0.5381, 0.1856,-0.4319), float3( 0.1379, 0.2486, 0.4430),
[21679]     float3( 0.3371, 0.5679,-0.0057), float3(-0.6999,-0.0451,-0.0019),
[21680]     float3( 0.0689,-0.1598,-0.8547), float3( 0.0560, 0.0069,-0.1843),
[21681]     float3(-0.0146, 0.1402, 0.0762), float3( 0.0100,-0.1924,-0.0344),
[21682]     float3(-0.3577,-0.5301,-0.4358), float3(-0.3169, 0.1063, 0.0158),
[21683]     float3( 0.0103,-0.5869, 0.0046), float3(-0.0897,-0.4940, 0.3287),
[21684]     float3( 0.7119,-0.0154,-0.0918), float3(-0.0533, 0.0596,-0.5411),
[21685]     float3( 0.0352,-0.0631, 0.5460), float3(-0.4776, 0.2847,-0.0271)
[21686] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21687]     float depth = texDepth.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0);
[21688]     float linearDepth = linearizeDepth(depth);
[21689]     // Skip sky (very far pixels)
[21690]     if (linearDepth > farPlane * 0.95)
[21691]         return float4(1, 1, 1, 1);
[21692]     // Skip very close geometry (truck interior, cockpit < 3m)
[21693]     // These surfaces occlude each other heavily and produce black artifacts
[21694]     if (linearDepth < 3.0)
[21695]         return float4(1, 1, 1, 1);
[21696]     // Random rotation for this pixel
[21697]     float2 rnd = noise(pos.xy);
[21698]     float  angle = rnd.x * 6.2831853;
[21699]     float  cs = cos(angle), sn = sin(angle);
[21700]     float2x2 rotMat = float2x2(cs, -sn, sn, cs);
[21701]     // Scale radius by depth 
[21702]  clamp tightly to avoid giant halos on close objects
[21703]     float screenRadius = radius / max(linearDepth, 0.5);
[21704]     screenRadius = clamp(screenRadius, 0.002, 0.06); // max 6% of screen
[21705]     // Depth-scaled bias: more bias for close objects (prevents self-occlusion)
[21706]     float scaledBias = bias * (1.0 + 4.0 / max(linearDepth, 1.0));
[21707]     float occlusion = 0.0;
[21708]     int samples = clamp(sampleCount, 4, 16);
[21709]     [loop]
[21710]     for (int i = 0; i < samples; i++) {
[21711]         // Rotate kernel sample
[21712]         float2 sampleOffset = mul(rotMat, kernel[i].xy) * screenRadius;
[21713]         float2 sampleUV = uv + sampleOffset;
[21714]         // Clamp to valid UV
[21715]         sampleUV = clamp(sampleUV, texelSize, 1.0 - texelSize);
[21716]         float sampleDepth = texDepth.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, sampleUV, 0);
[21717]         float sampleLinear = linearizeDepth(sampleDepth);
[21718]         // Only occlude if sample is closer than current pixel (in front)
[21719]         float depthDiff = linearDepth - sampleLinear;
[21720]         // Range check: soft falloff within the world-space radius 
[21721]  zero at boundary
[21722]         // This prevents halos at depth discontinuities (truck vs sky)
[21723]         float rangeCheck = smoothstep(radius, 0.0, abs(depthDiff));
[21724]         occlusion += (depthDiff > scaledBias) ? rangeCheck : 0.0;
[21725]     }
[21726]     occlusion = 1.0 - (occlusion / float(samples)) * intensity;
[21727]     // Hard floor: AO never darkens below 40% 
[21728]  prevents full-black artifacts
[21729]     occlusion = clamp(occlusion, 0.40, 1.0);
[21730]     return float4(occlusion, occlusion, occlusion, 1.0);
[21731] cbuffer BlurCB : register(b0) {
[21732]     float2 texelSize;
[21733]     float2 direction;   // (1,0) or (0,1)
[21734] SamplerState sPointClamp  : register(s0);
[21735] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s1);
[21736] Texture2D<float4> texAO    : register(t0);
[21737] Texture2D<float>  texDepth : register(t1);
[21738] static const float weights[5] = { 0.2270270, 0.1945945, 0.1216216, 0.0540540, 0.0162162 };
[21739] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21740]     float centerAO    = texAO.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0).r;
[21741]     float centerDepth = texDepth.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0);
[21742]     float2 blurStep = texelSize * direction;
[21743]     float totalAO     = centerAO * weights[0];
[21744]     float totalWeight = weights[0];
[21745]     [unroll]
[21746]     for (int i = 1; i < 5; i++) {
[21747]         float2 offset = blurStep * float(i);
[21748]         // Positive direction
[21749]         float2 uvP = uv + offset;
[21750]         float aoP    = texAO.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uvP, 0).r;
[21751]         float depthP = texDepth.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uvP, 0);
[21752]         // Soft depth-aware weight: exponential falloff instead of hard threshold
[21753]         // Factor 300 tuned for reversed-Z: kills blur across edges, smooth elsewhere
[21754]         float wP = weights[i] * exp(-abs(depthP - centerDepth) * 300.0);
[21755]         totalAO     += aoP * wP;
[21756]         totalWeight += wP;
[21757]         // Negative direction
[21758]         float2 uvN = uv - offset;
[21759]         float aoN    = texAO.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uvN, 0).r;
[21760]         float depthN = texDepth.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uvN, 0);
[21761]         float wN = weights[i] * exp(-abs(depthN - centerDepth) * 300.0);
[21762]         totalAO     += aoN * wN;
[21763]         totalWeight += wN;
[21764]     }
[21765]     return float4(totalAO / max(totalWeight, 0.001), 0, 0, 1);
[21766] cbuffer CompositeCB : register(b0) {
[21767]     float intensity;
[21768]     float3 _pad;
[21769] SamplerState sPointClamp  : register(s0);
[21770] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s1);
[21771] Texture2D<float4> texScene : register(t0);   // full res scene
[21772] Texture2D<float4> texAO    : register(t1);   // half res AO (bilinear upsampled)
[21773] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21774]     float3 scene = texScene.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0).rgb;
[21775]     float ao = texAO.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv, 0).r;
[21776]     // Modulate: lerp between full scene and darkened scene
[21777]     float aoFactor = lerp(1.0, ao, intensity);
[21778]     return float4(scene * aoFactor, 1.0);
[21779] cbuffer ThresholdCB : register(b0) {
[21780]     float2 texelSize;     // 1/fullWidth, 1/fullHeight
[21781]     float  threshold;
[21782]     float  softThreshold; // knee
[21783] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s0);
[21784] Texture2D<float4> texColor : register(t0);
[21785] float getLuma(float3 c) { return dot(c, float3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722)); }
[21786] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21787]     // 4-tap box downsample (bilinear filter does 2x2 average, offset to centers)
[21788]     float3 c0 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-0.5, -0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21789]     float3 c1 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( 0.5, -0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21790]     float3 c2 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2(-0.5,  0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21791]     float3 c3 = texColor.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + float2( 0.5,  0.5) * texelSize, 0).rgb;
[21792]     float3 color = (c0 + c1 + c2 + c3) * 0.25;
[21793]     // Soft threshold (quadratic knee)
[21794]     float luma = getLuma(color);
[21795]     float knee = threshold * softThreshold;
[21796]     float soft = luma - threshold + knee;
[21797]     soft = clamp(soft, 0.0, 2.0 * knee);
[21798]     soft = (knee > 0.0001) ? (soft * soft) / (4.0 * knee) : 0.0;
[21799]     float contribution = max(soft, luma - threshold);
[21800]     contribution = max(contribution, 0.0) / max(luma, 0.0001);
[21801]     return float4(color * contribution, 1.0);
[21802] cbuffer BlurCB : register(b0) {
[21803]     float2 texelSize;     // 1/bloomWidth, 1/bloomHeight
[21804]     float2 direction;     // (1,0) or (0,1)
[21805] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s0);
[21806] Texture2D<float4> texBloom : register(t0);
[21807] // 9-tap gaussian weights (sigma ~2.5, normalized)
[21808] static const float weights[5] = { 0.2270270, 0.1945945, 0.1216216, 0.0540540, 0.0162162 };
[21809] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21810]     float2 step = texelSize * direction;
[21811]     float3 result = texBloom.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv, 0).rgb * weights[0];
[21812]     [unroll]
[21813]     for (int i = 1; i < 5; i++) {
[21814]         float2 offset = step * float(i);
[21815]         result += texBloom.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv + offset, 0).rgb * weights[i];
[21816]         result += texBloom.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv - offset, 0).rgb * weights[i];
[21817]     }
[21818]     return float4(result, 1.0);
[21819] cbuffer CompositeCB : register(b0) {
[21820]     float intensity;
[21821]     float3 _pad;
[21822] SamplerState sPointClamp  : register(s0);
[21823] SamplerState sLinearClamp : register(s1);
[21824] Texture2D<float4> texScene : register(t0);  // original scene (full res)
[21825] Texture2D<float4> texBloom : register(t1);  // blurred bloom (1/4 res, upsampled via bilinear)
[21826] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target0 {
[21827]     float3 scene = texScene.SampleLevel(sPointClamp, uv, 0).rgb;
[21828]     float3 bloom = texBloom.SampleLevel(sLinearClamp, uv, 0).rgb;
[21829]     // Additive blend with intensity control
[21830]     float3 result = scene + bloom * intensity;
[21831]     return float4(saturate(result), 1.0);
[21832] float4 main(uint vid : SV_VertexID) : SV_Position {
[21833]     float2 uv = float2((vid & 1) ? 2.0f : 0.0f, (vid & 2) ? 2.0f : 0.0f);
[21834]     return float4(uv.x * 2.0f - 1.0f, 1.0f - uv.y * 2.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f);
[21835] cbuffer ColorCB : register(b0) {

[21838]     return gColor;

[21845]     VSOut o;

[21848]     return o;
[21849] Texture2D    gTex     : register(t0);

[21859] Texture2D    gGps     : register(t0);
[21860] Texture2D    gCef     : register(t1);
[21861] SamplerState gSampler : register(s0);
[21862] cbuffer SplitCB : register(b0) {
[21863]     float4 splitParams;
[21864] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position, float2 uv : TEXCOORD0) : SV_Target {
[21865]     float ratio    = splitParams.x;
[21866]     float gpsFirst = splitParams.y;
[21867]     float vertical = splitParams.z;
[21868]     float flipV    = splitParams.w;              // telas cujo mesh 
[21869]  de cabe
[21870] a p/ baixo (ex.: Compacta)
[21871]     if (flipV > 0.5f) uv.y = 1.0f - uv.y;        // espelha V p/ compensar (igual ao PS texturado flip)
[21872]     float3 color;
[21873]     if (vertical > 0.5f) {
[21874]         if (gpsFirst > 0.5f) {
[21875]             if (uv.y < ratio) { color = gGps.Sample(gSampler, float2(uv.x, uv.y / ratio)).rgb; }
[21876]             else               { color = gCef.Sample(gSampler, float2(uv.x, (uv.y - ratio) / (1.0f - ratio))).rgb; }
[21877]         } else {
[21878]             if (uv.y < (1.0f - ratio)) { color = gCef.Sample(gSampler, float2(uv.x, uv.y / (1.0f - ratio))).rgb; }
[21879]             else                        { color = gGps.Sample(gSampler, float2(uv.x, (uv.y - (1.0f - ratio)) / ratio)).rgb; }
[21880]         }
[21881]     } else {
[21882]         if (gpsFirst > 0.5f) {
[21883]             if (uv.x < ratio) { color = gGps.Sample(gSampler, float2(uv.x / ratio, uv.y)).rgb; }
[21884]             else               { color = gCef.Sample(gSampler, float2((uv.x - ratio) / (1.0f - ratio), uv.y)).rgb; }
[21885]         } else {
[21886]             if (uv.x < (1.0f - ratio)) { color = gCef.Sample(gSampler, float2(uv.x / (1.0f - ratio), uv.y)).rgb; }
[21887]             else                        { color = gGps.Sample(gSampler, float2((uv.x - (1.0f - ratio)) / ratio, uv.y)).rgb; }
[21888]         }
[21889]     }
[21890]     return float4(color, 1.0f);
[21891] cbuffer CursorCB : register(b0) {
[21892]     float4 cursorData;   // x=cursorX, y=cursorY, z=outerRadius, w=colorR
[21893]     float4 rtData;       // x=rtWidth, y=rtHeight, z=colorG, w=colorB
[21894] float4 main(float4 pos : SV_Position) : SV_Target {
[21895]     float2 delta = pos.xy - cursorData.xy;
[21896]     float  dist  = length(delta);
[21897]     float  R     = cursorData.z;   // raio externo (8px)
[21898]     float  borderW = 2.0f;         // espessura da borda escura
[21899]     float  Ri    = R - borderW;    // raio do interior branco
[21900]     // smoothstep(a,b,x) com a>b inverte: 1 dentro, 0 fora, transi
[21901] o suave de ~1.5px.
[21902]     float edge = smoothstep(R + 0.75f, R - 0.75f, dist);   // m
[21903] scara da bolinha (anti-alias)
[21904]     clip(edge - 0.001f);                                    // descarta tudo fora (n
[21905] o grava no RT)
[21906]     float innerMask = smoothstep(Ri + 0.75f, Ri - 0.75f, dist); // 1=interior, 0=borda
[21907]     float3 innerColor  = float3(cursorData.w, rtData.z, rtData.w);  // cor configuravel (default branco)
[21908]     float3 borderColor = float3(0.08f, 0.08f, 0.08f);  // quase-preto (contorno fixo p/ contraste)
[21909]     float3 color = lerp(borderColor, innerColor, innerMask);
[21910]     float baseAlpha  = lerp(0.85f, 0.92f, innerMask);  // borda 0.85, interior 0.92
[21911]     float finalAlpha = baseAlpha * edge;               // suaviza a borda externa
[21912]     return float4(color, finalAlpha);
